# M37 expanded source and comparison-mode audit

HEAD `2b1aca18faeef72a5e090dbc3af4ba6151b4ae80`, parent `c3040c184ad4da0fc638d61ab94d46501c61a11a`. No blocking code or new data-identity defect established in this lane. All generated scripts, outputs and temporary-file roots are under `audit_evidence_claims/m37/source/`. Production code was not edited. See [independent Spec axis](spec_axis.md).

## Expanded source universe

- **22,956 distinct inputs from 34,429 records**, including **every one of 9,581 dictionary drug alias entries**, preferred names, index aliases and canonical index keys, SQL node labels, target symbols, company keys and disease keys.
- 16,015 literal source spellings/read keys; 6,941 additional canonical-only index strings. 6,469 live SQL nodes.
- Fresh Engine responses: `alias_only` 12,486, `center` 10,088, `refused` 354, `choose` 8, `not_found` 5, `not_one_name` 15. Independent membership, refused-stored-spelling closure, silent-ambiguity and live-choice assertions: **0 failures / 0 exceptions**. Elapsed 947.748 seconds.
- **0 source-response changes** from the saved M36 source sweep. This is a current-versus-saved-prior-response comparison, not a newly executed parent Engine run.
- The source audit rebuilds allowed/read membership from dictionary and SQL records. Its independent Unicode state-machine oracle does not import `_sig` or inspect `Engine.names`. No label/preferred-name discrepancy was found; 1,696 aliases omitted by the lookup index are present in the expanded dictionary-alias inventory.

Artifacts: [input provenance](all_source_records.json), [source responses](source_sweep.json), [summary/assertions](source_summary.json), [source classes](source_classified_summary.json), [prior-response comparison](source_parent_changes.json), [source differences](source_discrepancies.json), [reproducible audit](source_audit.py).

## Two comparison modes and their boundary

The independent oracle implements the specified preprocessing in order, then scans Unicode categories with a state machine. It does not reuse the production bracket regex. A strict signature retains at least one opening (`Ps`/`Pi`) and closing (`Pe`/`Pf`) punctuation character. An ordinary signature contains no category-P character. Their output spaces are therefore disjoint; equality between different modes cannot draw a wrong node. This argument concerns the stated algorithm, not every possible scientific interpretation of arbitrary user text.

**321,384 comparisons** cover 22,956 source inputs and 14 forms: literal, swapcase, combining mark, invisible characters, spaces, trademarks, fullwidth alphanumerics, inserted slashes, inserted underscores, surrounding brackets, empty-group prefix, symbol-group prefix, minus/underscore/mark prefix, and dash substitution. Independent-oracle mismatches: **0**. The source has **14 distinct strict-mode spellings / 30 source occurrences** and **22,942 ordinary-mode spellings**. Forty-two expected signature differences are the three punctuation-altering transforms applied to 14 strict spellings.

The raw generic-invariance probe also reports two swapcase differences, both containing Turkish dotless U+0131: `Econix Araştırma Analiz Ve Danışmanlık` and `Prof Dr Cemil Tascıoglu Education And Research Hospital Organization`. Their uppercase-I forms have different Unicode default casefolds and return `not_found`; the source forms draw their own organization IDs. This satisfies the explicitly specified casefold algorithm, is inherited behavior, and is not an oracle failure or a demonstrated wrong-node collision. Raw results and fresh HTTP responses are retained without rewriting the probe history.

- All **11,086 assigned punctuation/mark/symbol/separator codepoints × 4 contexts = 44,344** comparisons pass; zero plain-ARTEMISININ collisions in the checked negative/empty-group forms.
- All **91 openers × 87 closers = 7,917** Unicode delimiter pairs pass, including mismatched delimiter types.
- All **138,557 assigned letter/number codepoints** in `(-<character>)ARTEMISININ` match the independent oracle. Sixteen lose their letters/numbers during required preprocessing and correctly activate strict comparison.
- Fresh direct follow-up covers 103 inputs; **28 HTTP records** cover nested/mixed/mismatched delimiters, quotations, preprocessing-created groups, alternate descriptor spellings, dash equivalence and the two casefold recall probes.

| Exact input | Fresh HTTP result | Classifier explanation |
|---|---|---|
| `((-))ARTEMISININ`, `([-])ARTEMISININ`, `[(-])ARTEMISININ` | `not_one_name` | An inner qualifying group activates literal comparison; brackets/signs survive. |
| `«-〕ARTEMISININ` | `not_one_name` | Any stated opening/closing categories count, even mixed delimiter types. |
| `(+ / −)NICOTINE` | CHEMBL440464 | Spaces and dash style are the explicitly allowed strict-mode equivalences. |
| `[+/-]NICOTINE`, `((+/-))NICOTINE`, `(+/-)NICO/TINE` | `not_found` | Strict punctuation differs from the stored `(+/-)NICOTINE`. |
| `(®)osimertinib`, `(™)osimertinib` | `not_one_name`, `not_found` | Removing trademark signs leaves an empty group and activates strict mode. |
| `(-ARTEMISININ)`, `(-ARTEMISININ`, `(-A)RTEMISININ` | CHEMBL269671 | No complete bracketed group containing zero letters/numbers exists. These use the explicit ordinary-mode punctuation rule. |
| `'-'ARTEMISININ`, `"-"ARTEMISININ` | CHEMBL269671 | ASCII quotation marks are category `Po`, not the specified `Pi`/`Pf` pair. |

The last two rows are deliberate boundary probes, **not claims of a conventional isolated `(-)` descriptor being erased**, and not new blockers under the explicit M37 classifier. Reverse-oriented smart quotes also remain ordinary; the opening-then-closing version is strict. No scientific identity conclusion is inferred from malformed/partial descriptor-looking text.

Artifacts: [composition summary](source_mode_summary.json), [strict source records](source_mode_inventory.json), [expected strict differences](source_mode_differences.json), [probe classification](mode_probe_classification.json), [Unicode symbols/punctuation](unicode_mode_summary.json), [letters/numbers](letter_number_classification.json), [direct follow-up](mode_followup.json), [HTTP boundary cases](mode_boundary_http.json), [scripts](modes.py), [boundary script](mode_boundaries.py).

## Refusal, extra symbols and retirement

- **182 literal refused source spellings × 13 forms = 2,366**: all refused.
- **84 canonical-only keys × 13 = 1,092**: 1,074 refused, 12 `alias_only`, six `not_found`, zero centers. These 18 different terminal states are synthetic-key probes, not literal stored-name refusal failures. The unclassified raw script output is preserved and this distinction is explicit in [classified results](refusal_variants_classified.json).
- **1,066 preferred names × six significant suffixes = 6,396**: zero centers. Suffixes were `+`, `$`, `=`, radiation sign U+2622, Greek gamma U+03B3 and CJK U+4E2D.
- **54 private retirement cases / 10 assertion groups** pass: two live candidates, one live candidate, all candidates retired, lowercase IDs, and retired Roche. Both indexes created before/after retirement were checked. All mutations used private in-memory SQLite copies; production DB SHA-256 is unchanged.

Artifacts: [refusal raw responses](refusal_variants.json), [significant suffixes](significant_suffixes_summary.json), [private retirement fixtures](retired_private_fixture.json).

## Code notes versus data notes

**Code/documentation should-fix:** `Engine.explore` at `src/app.py:110-112` still says punctuation is ignored without stating the descriptor exception. The detailed `_sig` docstring and M37 brief describe the two-mode rule correctly. `(+/-)NICOTINE` versus `(+/-)NICO/TINE` is an exact counterexample to the short universal wording; it is an intended fail-closed recall difference and not a wrong drawing.

**Data:** no additional admitted-name scientific identity error was established. Source inventory and membership consistency are not an exhaustive scientific identity certification. Existing excluded aliases, brands, the tracer labels, preferred-name over-refusals, resolver lists and THEOPHYLLINE type remain the recorded limitations; this review makes no curation change.

All finite-test counts above are bounded to the enumerated inputs and fixtures. No universal free-text refusal or source identity certification is claimed.

# Spec axis (independent lane)

No blocking specification violation established in the M37 diff (`git diff HEAD^...HEAD`, HEAD `2b1aca18faeef72a5e090dbc3af4ba6151b4ae80`).

The M37 brief requires that a name with a qualifying bracketed group be "compared character for character" after the stated preprocessing, while "Every other name keeps the round-32 rule." The implementation satisfies the independent state-machine oracle for the source composition, Unicode punctuation/symbol/mark/separator, delimiter-pair, and letter/number probes retained in this directory. This oracle does not call `app._sig` or read `Engine.names`. Strict-mode signatures retain an opening and closing punctuation character; ordinary-mode signatures retain no punctuation. Therefore the two modes cannot themselves collide after their specified transformations. Direct Engine and HTTP examples verify nesting, mixed delimiter types, preprocessing-created empty groups, dash equivalence, symbol preservation, and intentionally excluded alternate descriptor spellings.

The inherited requirement "A refused stored name stays refused, however it is spelt" passes for all 182 literal refused source spellings across 13 tested forms. Eighteen terminal-state differences belong to synthetic canonical-only keys, not stored literal names; none draws a center. Significant suffix and private retirement tests likewise establish no new violation. Data identity errors are not inferred merely from an alias or a canonical-key collision.

One nonblocking wording mismatch remains: `Engine.explore` (`src/app.py:110-112`) still describes comparison as "punctuation ... aside" without the descriptor-mode exception. For example, stored `(+/-)NICOTINE` draws, but `(+/-)NICO/TINE` does not. The detailed `_sig` docstring and M37 brief state the new behavior correctly; update the shorter wording to avoid promising punctuation-insensitive recall for descriptor names. No scope creep identified.

+## Independent attack on the two comparison modes
+
+The oracle reconstructs names and candidate IDs from the dictionary and SQL records; it does not use `Engine.names`. Its normalization classifier is a separate character scanner rather than the implementation's `_GROUP` regular expression. [Oracle and source sweep](source/source_audit.py), [composition probes](source/modes.py), [boundary probes](source/mode_boundaries.py).
+
+The critical separation is structural: a strict-mode signature retains at least one opening/initial-quotation and one closing/final-quotation punctuation character. An ordinary-mode signature removes all punctuation. Those signature spaces cannot collide. This argument depends on the promised preprocessing order: trademark removal, compatibility/case folding and mark/invisible removal precede classification; space removal and dash normalization cannot erase those retained delimiters. It supports mode separation, not universal chemical equivalence of ordinary punctuation-normalized names.
+
+- **22,956 source/key inputs × 14 transforms = 321,384 comparisons.** Fourteen literal source spellings use strict mode; 22,942 use ordinary mode. No implementation-versus-independent-oracle mismatch was found. There are 42 mode-dependent punctuation-transform differences, which are expected under the new policy. Two additional generic `swapcase()` invariance probes differ for Turkish dotless `ı`; both agree with the prescribed casefold oracle. Those probe assumptions are not silently counted as passing or as new map defects. [Summary](source/source_mode_summary.json), [differences](source/source_mode_differences.json), [follow-up outcomes](source/mode_followup.json).
+- **11,086 assigned punctuation/mark/symbol/space characters × four contexts = 44,344 cases**, plus all **91 opening × 87 closing delimiters = 7,917 pairs**: zero oracle failures or unsafe plain-ARTEMISININ collisions. [Unicode summary](source/unicode_mode_summary.json).
+- **138,557 assigned Unicode letter/number characters** inserted into a candidate group: zero classifier disagreements. Sixteen lose their letter/number category during the specified compatibility/mark preprocessing and correctly change mode afterwards. [Classification results](source/letter_number_classification.json).
+- Nested and mismatched brackets, Unicode quotations, empty groups, fullwidth forms, signs, unrelated symbols, letters/digits inside a group and preprocessing-created empty groups were exercised. **28 HTTP boundary controls** supplement root's confirmations. `(-ARTEMISININ)` and `(-A)RTEMISININ` have letters inside their only group and therefore use ordinary mode, drawing ARTEMISININ; ASCII apostrophes are category Po, not the designated Pi/Pf quotation delimiters. These results follow the explicit new classifier and inherited ordinary punctuation rule. They are disclosed design boundaries, not evidence that the code interprets arbitrary strings as optical descriptors. [Boundary evidence](source/mode_boundary_http.json).
+- **182 literal refused spellings × 13 variants = 2,366, all refused.** A separate 84 synthetic canonical-only keys × 13 = 1,092 cases yield 1,074 refusals, 12 `alias_only`, six `not_found`, no centers. All **1,066 preferred names × six significant suffixes = 6,396** remain undrawn. **54 private retirement cases / ten assertion groups** pass. [Refusal summary](source/refusal_variants_summary.json), [suffixes](source/significant_suffixes_summary.json), [retirement fixture](source/retired_private_fixture.json).

+## New nonblocking findings
+
+### M37-S1 — punctuation or a symbol can end the question descriptor scan before its sign
+
+**Question-linker regression**, `src/query.py:118` and `src/query.py:133–135`. The parent accepted arbitrary nonword characters inside a sign group. The new adjacent run excludes punctuation such as `.` and symbols such as `☢` and `$`. Looking left from NICOTINE, the scan stops on that character before reaching the sign.
+
+| Exact input | Parent question result | HEAD question result | HEAD map |
+|---|---|---|---|
+| `What trials evaluate (+/-.)NICOTINE?` | no entity/evidence | CHEMBL3; two evidence rows | `not_one_name` |
+| `What trials evaluate (+/-☢)NICOTINE?` | no entity/evidence | CHEMBL3; two evidence rows | `not_one_name` |
+| `What trials evaluate (+/-$)NICOTINE?` | no entity/evidence | CHEMBL3; two evidence rows | `not_one_name` |
+| `What trials evaluate NICOTINE(.+/-)?` | no entity/evidence | CHEMBL3; two evidence rows | `not_one_name` |
+
+The same new reading occurs with `×`, `:` and `,`, including mirrored suffix forms. The combined **28-question / 112-HTTP** probe has **12 newly admitted question readings** and no map centers. `/api/ask` and `/api/check` expose the entity; `/api/structured` retrieves the same two evidence rows. The opposite-side punctuation controls remain blocked because the scan meets a sign first. This is a loss of question refusal coverage, not a map admission failure; it is nonblocking under README defect 15 and the explicit review scope. [Prefix/suffix evidence](contract/question_punctuation_regression.json), [mirrored suffix evidence](contract/question_punctuation_mirror.json).
+
+### M37-S2 — ordinary sign-separated lists lose a shared preferred name
+
+**Question recall/design cost**, `src/query.py:133–135`. `What trials evaluate NICOTINE + osimertinib?` previously linked CHEMBL3 and CHEMBL3353410; now only CHEMBL3353410 remains. The selected evidence consequently changes from two NICOTINE rows to 12 osimertinib rows. `What trials evaluate osimertinib + NICOTINE?` and `What trials evaluate NICOTINE - osimertinib?` also drop NICOTINE. The `/` and `and` controls retain both entities.
+
+This follows the requested rule that **any adjacent sign** blocks a shared-key preferred-name reading. It should be understood as a recall tradeoff, rather than claimed to violate that new rule. All such complete questions remain undrawn by the map. The expanded supplement tests **six names × 30 patterns × two wrappers = 360 cases**, with 74 parent-query changes and zero map centers; not all 74 changes are defects. [Supplement and parent comparison](contract/question_supplement.json), [summary](contract/question_supplement_summary.json), [HTTP](contract/question_supplement_http.json).
+
+Previously existing free-text leaks are separate: `What trials evaluate 〔+/-〕NICOTINE?`, `What trials evaluate NICOTINE«+/-»?` and `"What trials evaluate (+/-)\u200bNICOTINE?"` still read CHEMBL3 with two evidence rows, identically to the parent. These remain recorded question-box limitations. Ordinary `(NICOTINE)` recall and the exact M36 nested-descriptor repair pass.
+
+### M37-S3 — page and explore documentation omit the new punctuation exception
+
+**Documentation should-fix**, `web/try.html:54` and `src/app.py:110–115`. Their unqualified claim that punctuation is ignored no longer describes every name. The new `_sig` docstring explains the exception, but the visible page does not. For example, `(+/-)NICOTINE` draws while `(+/-)NICO/TINE`, `(+/-)NICOTINE!`, or bracket changes do not; `()osimertinib` is also undrawn. Explain that a bracketed group without letters/digits switches the whole name to literal-punctuation comparison. These are accepted recall exclusions, so the wording issue does not negate the map safety result.

+import importlib.util, json, pathlib, subprocess, sys, urllib.request
+
+ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+OUT = ROOT / 'audit_evidence_claims/m37'
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'src'))
+import app
+
+def save(name, value):
+    (OUT / name).write_text(json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+
+def load_parent(name):
+    dest = OUT / ('parent_' + name + '.py')
+    dest.write_bytes(subprocess.check_output(['git', 'show', 'HEAD^:src/' + name + '.py'], cwd=ROOT))
+    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('m37_parent_' + name, dest)
+    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
+    sys.modules[spec.name] = mod
+    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
+    return mod
+
+parent_query = load_parent('query')
+current_query = sys.modules['query']
+try:
+    sys.modules['query'] = parent_query
+    parent_app = load_parent('app')
+finally:
+    sys.modules['query'] = current_query
+old = parent_app.Engine()
+now = app.Engine()
+
+def compact(body):
+    return {'state': next((k for k in ['center','refused','choose','alias_only','not_one_name','not_found','error'] if k in body), 'question'),
+            'center': body.get('center', {}).get('id'),
+            'choices': [c['id'] for c in body.get('choose', [])],
+            'entities': body.get('entities'), 'evidence_count': len(body.get('evidence', []))}
+
+families = {
+    'M36-B1': ['(-_)-ARTEMISININ', '(_-)-ARTEMISININ', '[-_]-ARTEMISININ',
+               '(-\u200b_\u0301)-ARTEMISININ', '(-\uff3f)-ARTEMISININ', '(-\ufe4d)-ARTEMISININ',
+               '(-())-ARTEMISININ', '\u3014-\u3015-ARTEMISININ', '\u00ab-\u00bb-ARTEMISININ'],
+    'M36-B2': ['(+/-\u2622)NICOTINE', '(\u00b1\u2622)NICOTINE', '(+/-$)NICOTINE',
+               '(+/-\u00d7)NICOTINE', '(+/\u2622-)NICOTINE', '(+/\u00d7-)NICOTINE'],
+    'M36-S1': ['What trials evaluate ((+/-))NICOTINE?', 'What does NICOTINE((+/-)) target?'],
+    'M35-B1': ['(-\u200b)-ARTEMISININ', '(-\ufeff)-ARTEMISININ', '(-\u2060)-ARTEMISININ', '(-\u0301)-ARTEMISININ'],
+    'M35-S1': ['What trials evaluate (+/-)(NICOTINE)?', 'What does (+/-)[NICOTINE] target?',
+               'What trials evaluate (NICOTINE)(+/-)?', 'What trials evaluate [NICOTINE](+/-)?'],
+    'descriptor-controls': ['ARTEMISININ', 'CHEMBL269671', '(+/-)NICOTINE', '( + / - )NICOTINE',
+               '(+/\u2013)NICOTINE', '(+/-)nicotine', '(\u00b1)-nicotine', '(+-)NICOTINE',
+               '(-+)-NICOTINE', '(+/-)NICOTINE\u2622', '()osimertinib', '(_-_)_-_P_A_C_L_I_T_A_X_E_L',
+               '(_-_)_-_g_o_s_s_y_p_o_l', '(_-_)_-_s_i_r_p_i_g_l_e_n_a_s_t_a_t'],
+    'mode-boundaries': ['(((-)))ARTEMISININ', '(a(-))ARTEMISININ', '(a-b)ARTEMISININ',
+               '\u2018-\u2019ARTEMISININ', '\u201c-\u201dARTEMISININ',
+               '[)-ARTEMISININ', '(]-ARTEMISININ', '(-\u200b_\u034f)-ARTEMISININ',
+               '(\u00ae)osimertinib', '(\u2122)osimertinib', '\uff08\uff0b/\uff0d\uff09NICOTINE',
+               '(+/-)N\u00cdCOTINE', '(\u200b+/\u0301-)NICOTINE'],
+}
+
+rows = []
+for family, inputs in families.items():
+    for text in inputs:
+        for route in (['/api/explore','/api/ask','/api/structured','/api/check'] if family in ['M36-S1','M35-S1'] else ['/api/explore']):
+            body = {'text': text}
+            if route == '/api/structured': body['answer'] = '{"claims":[]}'
+            if route == '/api/check': body['answer'] = 'NICOTINE is evaluated in trials.'
+            req = urllib.request.Request('http://127.0.0.1:8937' + route, data=json.dumps(body).encode(), headers={'Content-Type':'application/json'})
+            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=180) as r: result = json.load(r); status = r.status
+            rows.append({'family':family, 'input':text, 'escaped':text.encode('unicode_escape').decode(),
+                         'route':route,'request':body,'http_status':status,'response':result,
+                         'sig':app._sig(text),'parent_sig':parent_app._sig(text),
+                         'parent_map':compact(old.explore(text)),
+                         'current_linker':now.kg.link_entities(text),'parent_linker':old.kg.link_entities(text)})
+        save('root_round37_http.json', rows)
+summary = [dict(family=r['family'], input=r['input'], escaped=r['escaped'], route=r['route'],
+                current=compact(r['response']), parent_map=r['parent_map'],
+                parent_linker=r['parent_linker'], current_linker=r['current_linker']) for r in rows]
+save('root_round37_summary.json', summary)
+old.kg.conn.close()
+now.kg.conn.close()
+print(json.dumps({'http_records':len(rows), 'families':{k:len(v) for k,v in families.items()},
+                  'unexpected_M36_centers':[r['input'] for r in rows if r['family'] in ['M36-B1','M36-B2'] and 'center' in r['response']]}, ensure_ascii=False))

+## Independent attack on the two comparison modes
+
+The oracle reconstructs names and candidate IDs from the dictionary and SQL records; it does not use `Engine.names`. Its normalization classifier is a separate character scanner rather than the implementation's `_GROUP` regular expression. [Oracle and source sweep](source/source_audit.py), [composition probes](source/modes.py), [boundary probes](source/mode_boundaries.py).
+
+The critical separation is structural: a strict-mode signature retains at least one opening/initial-quotation and one closing/final-quotation punctuation character. An ordinary-mode signature removes all punctuation. Those signature spaces cannot collide. This argument depends on the promised preprocessing order: trademark removal, compatibility/case folding and mark/invisible removal precede classification; space removal and dash normalization cannot erase those retained delimiters. It supports mode separation, not universal chemical equivalence of ordinary punctuation-normalized names.
+
+- **22,956 source/key inputs × 14 transforms = 321,384 comparisons.** Fourteen literal source spellings use strict mode; 22,942 use ordinary mode. No implementation-versus-independent-oracle mismatch was found. There are 42 mode-dependent punctuation-transform differences, which are expected under the new policy. Two additional generic `swapcase()` invariance probes differ for Turkish dotless `ı`; both agree with the prescribed casefold oracle. Those probe assumptions are not silently counted as passing or as new map defects. [Summary](source/source_mode_summary.json), [differences](source/source_mode_differences.json), [follow-up outcomes](source/mode_followup.json).
+- **11,086 assigned punctuation/mark/symbol/space characters × four contexts = 44,344 cases**, plus all **91 opening × 87 closing delimiters = 7,917 pairs**: zero oracle failures or unsafe plain-ARTEMISININ collisions. [Unicode summary](source/unicode_mode_summary.json).
+- **138,557 assigned Unicode letter/number characters** inserted into a candidate group: zero classifier disagreements. Sixteen lose their letter/number category during the specified compatibility/mark preprocessing and correctly change mode afterwards. [Classification results](source/letter_number_classification.json).
+- Nested and mismatched brackets, Unicode quotations, empty groups, fullwidth forms, signs, unrelated symbols, letters/digits inside a group and preprocessing-created empty groups were exercised. **28 HTTP boundary controls** supplement root's confirmations. `(-ARTEMISININ)` and `(-A)RTEMISININ` have letters inside their only group and therefore use ordinary mode, drawing ARTEMISININ; ASCII apostrophes are category Po, not the designated Pi/Pf quotation delimiters. These results follow the explicit new classifier and inherited ordinary punctuation rule. They are disclosed design boundaries, not evidence that the code interprets arbitrary strings as optical descriptors. [Boundary evidence](source/mode_boundary_http.json).
+- **182 literal refused spellings × 13 variants = 2,366, all refused.** A separate 84 synthetic canonical-only keys × 13 = 1,092 cases yield 1,074 refusals, 12 `alias_only`, six `not_found`, no centers. All **1,066 preferred names × six significant suffixes = 6,396** remain undrawn. **54 private retirement cases / ten assertion groups** pass. [Refusal summary](source/refusal_variants_summary.json), [suffixes](source/significant_suffixes_summary.json), [retirement fixture](source/retired_private_fixture.json).

+## New nonblocking findings
+
+### M37-S1 — punctuation or a symbol can end the question descriptor scan before its sign
+
+**Question-linker regression**, `src/query.py:118` and `src/query.py:133–135`. The parent accepted arbitrary nonword characters inside a sign group. The new adjacent run excludes punctuation such as `.` and symbols such as `☢` and `$`. Looking left from NICOTINE, the scan stops on that character before reaching the sign.
+
+| Exact input | Parent question result | HEAD question result | HEAD map |
+|---|---|---|---|
+| `What trials evaluate (+/-.)NICOTINE?` | no entity/evidence | CHEMBL3; two evidence rows | `not_one_name` |
+| `What trials evaluate (+/-☢)NICOTINE?` | no entity/evidence | CHEMBL3; two evidence rows | `not_one_name` |
+| `What trials evaluate (+/-$)NICOTINE?` | no entity/evidence | CHEMBL3; two evidence rows | `not_one_name` |
+| `What trials evaluate NICOTINE(.+/-)?` | no entity/evidence | CHEMBL3; two evidence rows | `not_one_name` |
+
+The same new reading occurs with `×`, `:` and `,`, including mirrored suffix forms. The combined **28-question / 112-HTTP** probe has **12 newly admitted question readings** and no map centers. `/api/ask` and `/api/check` expose the entity; `/api/structured` retrieves the same two evidence rows. The opposite-side punctuation controls remain blocked because the scan meets a sign first. This is a loss of question refusal coverage, not a map admission failure; it is nonblocking under README defect 15 and the explicit review scope. [Prefix/suffix evidence](contract/question_punctuation_regression.json), [mirrored suffix evidence](contract/question_punctuation_mirror.json).
+
+### M37-S2 — ordinary sign-separated lists lose a shared preferred name
+
+**Question recall/design cost**, `src/query.py:133–135`. `What trials evaluate NICOTINE + osimertinib?` previously linked CHEMBL3 and CHEMBL3353410; now only CHEMBL3353410 remains. The selected evidence consequently changes from two NICOTINE rows to 12 osimertinib rows. `What trials evaluate osimertinib + NICOTINE?` and `What trials evaluate NICOTINE - osimertinib?` also drop NICOTINE. The `/` and `and` controls retain both entities.
+
+This follows the requested rule that **any adjacent sign** blocks a shared-key preferred-name reading. It should be understood as a recall tradeoff, rather than claimed to violate that new rule. All such complete questions remain undrawn by the map. The expanded supplement tests **six names × 30 patterns × two wrappers = 360 cases**, with 74 parent-query changes and zero map centers; not all 74 changes are defects. [Supplement and parent comparison](contract/question_supplement.json), [summary](contract/question_supplement_summary.json), [HTTP](contract/question_supplement_http.json).
+
+Previously existing free-text leaks are separate: `What trials evaluate 〔+/-〕NICOTINE?`, `What trials evaluate NICOTINE«+/-»?` and `"What trials evaluate (+/-)\u200bNICOTINE?"` still read CHEMBL3 with two evidence rows, identically to the parent. These remain recorded question-box limitations. Ordinary `(NICOTINE)` recall and the exact M36 nested-descriptor repair pass.
+
+### M37-S3 — page and explore documentation omit the new punctuation exception
+
+**Documentation should-fix**, `web/try.html:54` and `src/app.py:110–115`. Their unqualified claim that punctuation is ignored no longer describes every name. The new `_sig` docstring explains the exception, but the visible page does not. For example, `(+/-)NICOTINE` draws while `(+/-)NICO/TINE`, `(+/-)NICOTINE!`, or bracket changes do not; `()osimertinib` is also undrawn. Explain that a bracketed group without letters/digits switches the whole name to literal-punctuation comparison. These are accepted recall exclusions, so the wording issue does not negate the map safety result.
+

+def load_parent(name):
+    dest = OUT / ('parent_' + name + '.py')
+    dest.write_bytes(subprocess.check_output(['git', 'show', 'HEAD^:src/' + name + '.py'], cwd=ROOT))
+    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('m37_parent_' + name, dest)
+    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
+    sys.modules[spec.name] = mod
+    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
+    return mod
+
+parent_query = load_parent('query')
+current_query = sys.modules['query']

+port_results = {}

+    sock.close()

+## Independent attack on the two comparison modes
+
+The oracle reconstructs names and candidate IDs from the dictionary and SQL records; it does not use `Engine.names`. Its normalization classifier is a separate character scanner rather than the implementation's `_GROUP` regular expression. [Oracle and source sweep](source/source_audit.py), [composition probes](source/modes.py), [boundary probes](source/mode_boundaries.py).
+
+The critical separation is structural: a strict-mode signature retains at least one opening/initial-quotation and one closing/final-quotation punctuation character. An ordinary-mode signature removes all punctuation. Those signature spaces cannot collide. This argument depends on the promised preprocessing order: trademark removal, compatibility/case folding and mark/invisible removal precede classification; space removal and dash normalization cannot erase those retained delimiters. It supports mode separation, not universal chemical equivalence of ordinary punctuation-normalized names.
+
+- **22,956 source/key inputs × 14 transforms = 321,384 comparisons.** Fourteen literal source spellings use strict mode; 22,942 use ordinary mode. No implementation-versus-independent-oracle mismatch was found. There are 42 mode-dependent punctuation-transform differences, which are expected under the new policy. Two additional generic `swapcase()` invariance probes differ for Turkish dotless `ı`; both agree with the prescribed casefold oracle. Those probe assumptions are not silently counted as passing or as new map defects. [Summary](source/source_mode_summary.json), [differences](source/source_mode_differences.json), [follow-up outcomes](source/mode_followup.json).
+- **11,086 assigned punctuation/mark/symbol/space characters × four contexts = 44,344 cases**, plus all **91 opening × 87 closing delimiters = 7,917 pairs**: zero oracle failures or unsafe plain-ARTEMISININ collisions. [Unicode summary](source/unicode_mode_summary.json).
+- **138,557 assigned Unicode letter/number characters** inserted into a candidate group: zero classifier disagreements. Sixteen lose their letter/number category during the specified compatibility/mark preprocessing and correctly change mode afterwards. [Classification results](source/letter_number_classification.json).
+- Nested and mismatched brackets, Unicode quotations, empty groups, fullwidth forms, signs, unrelated symbols, letters/digits inside a group and preprocessing-created empty groups were exercised. **28 HTTP boundary controls** supplement root's confirmations. `(-ARTEMISININ)` and `(-A)RTEMISININ` have letters inside their only group and therefore use ordinary mode, drawing ARTEMISININ; ASCII apostrophes are category Po, not the designated Pi/Pf quotation delimiters. These results follow the explicit new classifier and inherited ordinary punctuation rule. They are disclosed design boundaries, not evidence that the code interprets arbitrary strings as optical descriptors. [Boundary evidence](source/mode_boundary_http.json).
+- **182 literal refused spellings × 13 variants = 2,366, all refused.** A separate 84 synthetic canonical-only keys × 13 = 1,092 cases yield 1,074 refusals, 12 `alias_only`, six `not_found`, no centers. All **1,066 preferred names × six significant suffixes = 6,396** remain undrawn. **54 private retirement cases / ten assertion groups** pass. [Refusal summary](source/refusal_variants_summary.json), [suffixes](source/significant_suffixes_summary.json), [retirement fixture](source/retired_private_fixture.json).

+## New nonblocking findings
+
+### M37-S1 — punctuation or a symbol can end the question descriptor scan before its sign
+
+**Question-linker regression**, `src/query.py:118` and `src/query.py:133–135`. The parent accepted arbitrary nonword characters inside a sign group. The new adjacent run excludes punctuation such as `.` and symbols such as `☢` and `$`. Looking left from NICOTINE, the scan stops on that character before reaching the sign.
+
+| Exact input | Parent question result | HEAD question result | HEAD map |
+|---|---|---|---|
+| `What trials evaluate (+/-.)NICOTINE?` | no entity/evidence | CHEMBL3; two evidence rows | `not_one_name` |
+| `What trials evaluate (+/-☢)NICOTINE?` | no entity/evidence | CHEMBL3; two evidence rows | `not_one_name` |
+| `What trials evaluate (+/-$)NICOTINE?` | no entity/evidence | CHEMBL3; two evidence rows | `not_one_name` |
+| `What trials evaluate NICOTINE(.+/-)?` | no entity/evidence | CHEMBL3; two evidence rows | `not_one_name` |
+
+The same new reading occurs with `×`, `:` and `,`, including mirrored suffix forms. The combined **28-question / 112-HTTP** probe has **12 newly admitted question readings** and no map centers. `/api/ask` and `/api/check` expose the entity; `/api/structured` retrieves the same two evidence rows. The opposite-side punctuation controls remain blocked because the scan meets a sign first. This is a loss of question refusal coverage, not a map admission failure; it is nonblocking under README defect 15 and the explicit review scope. [Prefix/suffix evidence](contract/question_punctuation_regression.json), [mirrored suffix evidence](contract/question_punctuation_mirror.json).
+
+### M37-S2 — ordinary sign-separated lists lose a shared preferred name
+
+**Question recall/design cost**, `src/query.py:133–135`. `What trials evaluate NICOTINE + osimertinib?` previously linked CHEMBL3 and CHEMBL3353410; now only CHEMBL3353410 remains. The selected evidence consequently changes from two NICOTINE rows to 12 osimertinib rows. `What trials evaluate osimertinib + NICOTINE?` and `What trials evaluate NICOTINE - osimertinib?` also drop NICOTINE. The `/` and `and` controls retain both entities.
+
+This follows the requested rule that **any adjacent sign** blocks a shared-key preferred-name reading. It should be understood as a recall tradeoff, rather than claimed to violate that new rule. All such complete questions remain undrawn by the map. The expanded supplement tests **six names × 30 patterns × two wrappers = 360 cases**, with 74 parent-query changes and zero map centers; not all 74 changes are defects. [Supplement and parent comparison](contract/question_supplement.json), [summary](contract/question_supplement_summary.json), [HTTP](contract/question_supplement_http.json).
+
+Previously existing free-text leaks are separate: `What trials evaluate 〔+/-〕NICOTINE?`, `What trials evaluate NICOTINE«+/-»?` and `"What trials evaluate (+/-)\u200bNICOTINE?"` still read CHEMBL3 with two evidence rows, identically to the parent. These remain recorded question-box limitations. Ordinary `(NICOTINE)` recall and the exact M36 nested-descriptor repair pass.
+
+### M37-S3 — page and explore documentation omit the new punctuation exception
+
+**Documentation should-fix**, `web/try.html:54` and `src/app.py:110–115`. Their unqualified claim that punctuation is ignored no longer describes every name. The new `_sig` docstring explains the exception, but the visible page does not. For example, `(+/-)NICOTINE` draws while `(+/-)NICO/TINE`, `(+/-)NICOTINE!`, or bracket changes do not; `()osimertinib` is also undrawn. Explain that a bracketed group without letters/digits switches the whole name to literal-punctuation comparison. These are accepted recall exclusions, so the wording issue does not negate the map safety result.

This is a bounded review result, not scientific certification of every source identity.

+import importlib.util, json, pathlib, subprocess, sys, urllib.request
+
+ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+OUT = ROOT / 'audit_evidence_claims/m37'
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'src'))
+import app
+
+def save(name, value):
+    (OUT / name).write_text(json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+
+def load_parent(name):
+    dest = OUT / ('parent_' + name + '.py')
+    dest.write_bytes(subprocess.check_output(['git', 'show', 'HEAD^:src/' + name + '.py'], cwd=ROOT))
+    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('m37_parent_' + name, dest)
+    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
+    sys.modules[spec.name] = mod
+    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
+    return mod
+
+parent_query = load_parent('query')
+current_query = sys.modules['query']
+try:
+    sys.modules['query'] = parent_query
+    parent_app = load_parent('app')
+finally:
+    sys.modules['query'] = current_query
+old = parent_app.Engine()
+now = app.Engine()
+
+def compact(body):
+    return {'state': next((k for k in ['center','refused','choose','alias_only','not_one_name','not_found','error'] if k in body), 'question'),
+            'center': body.get('center', {}).get('id'),
+            'choices': [c['id'] for c in body.get('choose', [])],
+            'entities': body.get('entities'), 'evidence_count': len(body.get('evidence', []))}
+
+families = {

+    'M35-B1': ['(-\u200b)-ARTEMISININ', '(-\ufeff)-ARTEMISININ', '(-\u2060)-ARTEMISININ', '(-\u0301)-ARTEMISININ'],
+    'M35-S1': ['What trials evaluate (+/-)(NICOTINE)?', 'What does (+/-)[NICOTINE] target?',
+               'What trials evaluate (NICOTINE)(+/-)?', 'What trials evaluate [NICOTINE](+/-)?'],
+    'descriptor-controls': ['ARTEMISININ', 'CHEMBL269671', '(+/-)NICOTINE', '( + / - )NICOTINE',
+               '(+/\u2013)NICOTINE', '(+/-)nicotine', '(\u00b1)-nicotine', '(+-)NICOTINE',
+               '(-+)-NICOTINE', '(+/-)NICOTINE\u2622', '()osimertinib', '(_-_)_-_P_A_C_L_I_T_A_X_E_L',
+               '(_-_)_-_g_o_s_s_y_p_o_l', '(_-_)_-_s_i_r_p_i_g_l_e_n_a_s_t_a_t'],
+    'mode-boundaries': ['(((-)))ARTEMISININ', '(a(-))ARTEMISININ', '(a-b)ARTEMISININ',
+               '\u2018-\u2019ARTEMISININ', '\u201c-\u201dARTEMISININ',
+               '[)-ARTEMISININ', '(]-ARTEMISININ', '(-\u200b_\u034f)-ARTEMISININ',
+               '(\u00ae)osimertinib', '(\u2122)osimertinib', '\uff08\uff0b/\uff0d\uff09NICOTINE',
+               '(+/-)N\u00cdCOTINE', '(\u200b+/\u0301-)NICOTINE'],
+}
+
+rows = []
+for family, inputs in families.items():
+    for text in inputs:
+        for route in (['/api/explore','/api/ask','/api/structured','/api/check'] if family in ['M36-S1','M35-S1'] else ['/api/explore']):
+            body = {'text': text}
+            if route == '/api/structured': body['answer'] = '{"claims":[]}'
+            if route == '/api/check': body['answer'] = 'NICOTINE is evaluated in trials.'
+            req = urllib.request.Request('http://127.0.0.1:8937' + route, data=json.dumps(body).encode(), headers={'Content-Type':'application/json'})
+            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=180) as r: result = json.load(r); status = r.status
+            rows.append({'family':family, 'input':text, 'escaped':text.encode('unicode_escape').decode(),

+                         'sig':app._sig(text),'parent_sig':parent_app._sig(text),

+import json, pathlib, re, socket, subprocess
+
+ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+OUT = ROOT / 'audit_evidence_claims/m37'
+report = (OUT / 'REPORT.md').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
+missing = []
+link_prose = re.sub(r'```.*?```|`[^`]*`', '', report, flags=re.S)
+for target in re.findall(r'\]\(([^)]+)\)', link_prose):
+    if '://' in target or target.startswith('#'):
+        continue
+    path = target.split('#')[0].strip('<>')
+    if path and not (OUT / path).exists():
+        missing.append(target)
+
+def read(name):
+    return json.loads((OUT / name).read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))
+
+integrity = read('integrity_final.json')
+source = read('source/source_summary.json')
+contract = read('contract/summary.json')
+test = read('pytest_meta.json')
+head = subprocess.check_output(['git', 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], cwd=ROOT).decode().strip()
+checks = {
+    'source_complete': source['source_names'] == 22956 and not source['failures'] and not source['errors'],
+    'contract_complete': contract['live_centers'] == 6469 and contract['db_errors'] == 0,
+    'pytest_exit_zero': test['exit_code'] == 0,
+    'head_unchanged': head == '2b1aca18faeef72a5e090dbc3af4ba6151b4ae80',
+    'tracked_and_data_unchanged': not integrity['changed'] and not integrity['added'] and not integrity['tracked_status'].strip(),
+    'no_missing_report_links': not missing,
+    'single_final_verdict': report.rstrip().endswith('VERDICT: APPROVE') and report.count('VERDICT:') == 1,
+    'no_draft_placeholders': not any(x in report for x in ['TODO', 'TBD', 'PLACEHOLDER']),
+}
+ports = [8937] + [int(p) for p in contract['ports_closed']]
+replay_summary = read('replay/execution_summary.json')
+for key in ['ports_closed', 'ports']:
+    values = replay_summary.get(key, {})
+    if isinstance(values, dict):
+        ports.extend(int(p) for p in values)
+    elif isinstance(values, list):
+        ports.extend(int(p) for p in values if isinstance(p, int) or str(p).isdigit())
+port_results = {}
+for port in sorted(set(ports)):
+    sock = socket.socket()
+    sock.settimeout(.5)
+    port_results[str(port)] = sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1', port)) != 0
+    sock.close()
+checks['checked_ports_closed'] = all(port_results.values())
+result = {'checks': checks, 'missing_links': missing, 'checked_ports_closed': port_results,
+          'report_bytes': (OUT / 'REPORT.md').stat().st_size, 'head': head}
+(OUT / 'report_validation.json').write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=True))
+assert all(checks.values()), result

# Historical replay evidence - round37

Reviewed HEAD `2b1aca18faeef72a5e090dbc3af4ba6151b4ae80`, parent `c3040c184ad4da0fc638d61ab94d46501c61a11a`. This lane establishes exact prior reproduction status under the current preferred-name policy. No new code blocker or upstream data identity defect was found in this lane.

## Exact recent findings

Every original M32-B1-B4/D1-D3, M33-B1/D1-D6/S1/S2, M34-B1/S1/S2, and M35-B1/S1 map-safety reproduction is FIXED under current policy. M34-D1 THEOPHYLLINE remains the recorded upstream `Protein` metadata defect. M32 Greek alias recall remains intentionally excluded. [Every exact input and disposition](recent_findings.md), [fresh assertions](exact_validation.json).

The 124 fresh recent map cases are retained in [M32](m32_exact_http.json), [M33](m33_exact_http.json), [M34](m34_exact_http.json), and [M35](m35_exact_http.json). All 456 original refused-brand variants refuse with no center. The eight Cabozantinib spelling variants refuse; its ID still works. Ordinary bracketed NICOTINE retains CHEMBL3 and two evidence rows in each question API. All six original M35-S1 decorated questions abstain; the two-drug positive control retains both entities.

The only semantic change in these recent cases and the inherited corpus is `(±)-nicotine`: CHEMBL440464 becomes `not_found`. Stored `(+/-)NICOTINE` still draws CHEMBL440464. This is expressly accepted descriptor-spelling exclusion under round37. Previous states are the saved round36 results, not a new execution of the parent tree. [Exact differences](parent_corpus_changes.json), [recent comparison](recent_parent_comparison.json).

## Corpus and inherited finding ledger

The fresh corpus has **4,703 distinct inputs**: all 4,689 prior inputs plus 14 exact M36 cases. States: `{"center": 300, "refused": 1434, "alias_only": 2555, "not_found": 73, "not_one_name": 337, "choose": 4}`. Independent membership, ambiguity, choice-set, and exception checks all have zero failures. [Summary](corpus_summary.json), [all replies](corpus_results.json), [oracle](corpus37.py).

The oracle independently scans normalized characters for an opening/closing Unicode punctuation pair enclosing no letter/number. It then applies the specified punctuation-preserving or punctuation-dropping comparison. It does not import app._sig or read Engine.names; membership is rebuilt from dictionary preferred names and live SQL labels, target symbols, company/disease keys. It is a corpus oracle, not proof over every Unicode input.

All **275 inherited naming rows** have fresh exact-case dispositions: **{'FIXED': 257, 'NOT FIXED': 18} overall**, **{'FIXED': 260, 'NOT FIXED': 15} for the map**. The 15 map residuals are nine recorded over-refusal rows and six excluded-alias recall cohorts; three additional overall residuals are question-only. [Readable ledger](historical_table.md), [full exact inputs and responses](per_finding_cases.json), [counts](matrix_summary.json). This inherited ledger does not include later M32-M36 findings, which have their separate exact case tables.

Fresh question controls cover 60 input strings and 116 questions. M31 leak/recall controls remain closed; comma-containing synonym behavior and the inherited negation/collateral-refusal question cohorts remain recorded NOT FIXED. [Question responses](qa_controls37.json).

**All 123 historical leaf jobs exited 0**, plus all 9 additional jobs. +## Independent attack on the two comparison modes
+
+The oracle reconstructs names and candidate IDs from the dictionary and SQL records; it does not use `Engine.names`. Its normalization classifier is a separate character scanner rather than the implementation's `_GROUP` regular expression. [Oracle and source sweep](source/source_audit.py), [composition probes](source/modes.py), [boundary probes](source/mode_boundaries.py).
+
+The critical separation is structural: a strict-mode signature retains at least one opening/initial-quotation and one closing/final-quotation punctuation character. An ordinary-mode signature removes all punctuation. Those signature spaces cannot collide. This argument depends on the promised preprocessing order: trademark removal, compatibility/case folding and mark/invisible removal precede classification; space removal and dash normalization cannot erase those retained delimiters. It supports mode separation, not universal chemical equivalence of ordinary punctuation-normalized names.
+
+- **22,956 source/key inputs × 14 transforms = 321,384 comparisons.** Fourteen literal source spellings use strict mode; 22,942 use ordinary mode. No implementation-versus-independent-oracle mismatch was found. There are 42 mode-dependent punctuation-transform differences, which are expected under the new policy. Two additional generic `swapcase()` invariance probes differ for Turkish dotless `ı`; both agree with the prescribed casefold oracle. Those probe assumptions are not silently counted as passing or as new map defects. [Summary](source/source_mode_summary.json), [differences](source/source_mode_differences.json), [follow-up outcomes](source/mode_followup.json).
+- **11,086 assigned punctuation/mark/symbol/space characters × four contexts = 44,344 cases**, plus all **91 opening × 87 closing delimiters = 7,917 pairs**: zero oracle failures or unsafe plain-ARTEMISININ collisions. [Unicode summary](source/unicode_mode_summary.json).
+- **138,557 assigned Unicode letter/number characters** inserted into a candidate group: zero classifier disagreements. Sixteen lose their letter/number category during the specified compatibility/mark preprocessing and correctly change mode afterwards. [Classification results](source/letter_number_classification.json).
+- Nested and mismatched brackets, Unicode quotations, empty groups, fullwidth forms, signs, unrelated symbols, letters/digits inside a group and preprocessing-created empty groups were exercised. **28 HTTP boundary controls** supplement root's confirmations. `(-ARTEMISININ)` and `(-A)RTEMISININ` have letters inside their only group and therefore use ordinary mode, drawing ARTEMISININ; ASCII apostrophes are category Po, not the designated Pi/Pf quotation delimiters. These results follow the explicit new classifier and inherited ordinary punctuation rule. They are disclosed design boundaries, not evidence that the code interprets arbitrary strings as optical descriptors. [Boundary evidence](source/mode_boundary_http.json).
+- **182 literal refused spellings × 13 variants = 2,366, all refused.** A separate 84 synthetic canonical-only keys × 13 = 1,092 cases yield 1,074 refusals, 12 `alias_only`, six `not_found`, no centers. All **1,066 preferred names × six significant suffixes = 6,396** remain undrawn. **54 private retirement cases / ten assertion groups** pass. [Refusal summary](source/refusal_variants_summary.json), [suffixes](source/significant_suffixes_summary.json), [retirement fixture](source/retired_private_fixture.json).

+## New nonblocking findings
+
+### M37-S1 — punctuation or a symbol can end the question descriptor scan before its sign
+
+**Question-linker regression**, `src/query.py:118` and `src/query.py:133–135`. The parent accepted arbitrary nonword characters inside a sign group. The new adjacent run excludes punctuation such as `.` and symbols such as `☢` and `$`. Looking left from NICOTINE, the scan stops on that character before reaching the sign.
+
+| Exact input | Parent question result | HEAD question result | HEAD map |
+|---|---|---|---|
+| `What trials evaluate (+/-.)NICOTINE?` | no entity/evidence | CHEMBL3; two evidence rows | `not_one_name` |

+| `What trials evaluate (+/-$)NICOTINE?` | no entity/evidence | CHEMBL3; two evidence rows | `not_one_name` |
+| `What trials evaluate NICOTINE(.+/-)?` | no entity/evidence | CHEMBL3; two evidence rows | `not_one_name` |
+
+The same new reading occurs with `×`, `:` and `,`, including mirrored suffix forms. The combined **28-question / 112-HTTP** probe has **12 newly admitted question readings** and no map centers. `/api/ask` and `/api/check` expose the entity; `/api/structured` retrieves the same two evidence rows. The opposite-side punctuation controls remain blocked because the scan meets a sign first. This is a loss of question refusal coverage, not a map admission failure; it is nonblocking under README defect 15 and the explicit review scope. [Prefix/suffix evidence](contract/question_punctuation_regression.json), [mirrored suffix evidence](contract/question_punctuation_mirror.json).

+### M37-S2 — ordinary sign-separated lists lose a shared preferred name
+
+**Question recall/design cost**, `src/query.py:133–135`. `What trials evaluate NICOTINE + osimertinib?` previously linked CHEMBL3 and CHEMBL3353410; now only CHEMBL3353410 remains. The selected evidence consequently changes from two NICOTINE rows to 12 osimertinib rows. `What trials evaluate osimertinib + NICOTINE?` and `What trials evaluate NICOTINE - osimertinib?` also drop NICOTINE. The `/` and `and` controls retain both entities.
+
+This follows the requested rule that **any adjacent sign** blocks a shared-key preferred-name reading. It should be understood as a recall tradeoff, rather than claimed to violate that new rule. All such complete questions remain undrawn by the map. The expanded supplement tests **six names × 30 patterns × two wrappers = 360 cases**, with 74 parent-query changes and zero map centers; not all 74 changes are defects. [Supplement and parent comparison](contract/question_supplement.json), [summary](contract/question_supplement_summary.json), [HTTP](contract/question_supplement_http.json).

These remain recorded question-box limitations. Ordinary `(NICOTINE)` recall and the exact M36 nested-descriptor repair pass.
+
+### M37-S3 — page and explore documentation omit the new punctuation exception
+
+**Documentation should-fix**, `web/try.html:54` and `src/app.py:110–115`. Their unqualified claim that punctuation is ignored no longer describes every name. The new `_sig` docstring explains the exception, but the visible page does not. For example, `(+/-)NICOTINE` draws while `(+/-)NICO/TINE`, `(+/-)NICOTINE!`, or bracket changes do not; `()osimertinib` is also undrawn. Explain that a bracketed group without letters/digits switches the whole name to literal-punctuation comparison. These are accepted recall exclusions, so the wording issue does not negate the map safety result.

+import importlib.util, json, pathlib, subprocess, sys, urllib.request
+
+ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+OUT = ROOT / 'audit_evidence_claims/m37'
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'src'))
+import app
+
+def save(name, value):
+    (OUT / name).write_text(json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+
+def load_parent(name):
+    dest = OUT / ('parent_' + name + '.py')
+    dest.write_bytes(subprocess.check_output(['git', 'show', 'HEAD^:src/' + name + '.py'], cwd=ROOT))
+    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('m37_parent_' + name, dest)
+    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
+    sys.modules[spec.name] = mod
+    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
+    return mod
+
+parent_query = load_parent('query')
+current_query = sys.modules['query']
+try:
+    sys.modules['query'] = parent_query
+    parent_app = load_parent('app')
+finally:
+    sys.modules['query'] = current_query
+old = parent_app.Engine()
+now = app.Engine()
+
+def compact(body):
+    return {'state': next((k for k in ['center','refused','choose','alias_only','not_one_name','not_found','error'] if k in body), 'question'),
+            'center': body.get('center', {}).get('id'),
+            'choices': [c['id'] for c in body.get('choose', [])],
+            'entities': body.get('entities'), 'evidence_count': len(body.get('evidence', []))}
+

+               '(+/-)N\u00cdCOTINE', '(\u200b+/\u0301-)NICOTINE'],

+rows = []

+            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=180) as r: result = json.load(r); status = r.status
+            rows.append({'family':family, 'input':text, 'escaped':text.encode('unicode_escape').decode(),

+import json, pathlib, re, socket, subprocess
+
+ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+OUT = ROOT / 'audit_evidence_claims/m37'
+report = (OUT / 'REPORT.md').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
+missing = []
+link_prose = re.sub(r'```.*?```|`[^`]*`', '', report, flags=re.S)
+for target in re.findall(r'\]\(([^)]+)\)', link_prose):
+    if '://' in target or target.startswith('#'):
+        continue
+    path = target.split('#')[0].strip('<>')
+    if path and not (OUT / path).exists():
+        missing.append(target)
+
+def read(name):
+    return json.loads((OUT / name).read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))
+
+integrity = read('integrity_final.json')
+source = read('source/source_summary.json')
+contract = read('contract/summary.json')
+test = read('pytest_meta.json')
+head = subprocess.check_output(['git', 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], cwd=ROOT).decode().strip()
+checks = {
+    'source_complete': source['source_names'] == 22956 and not source['failures'] and not source['errors'],
+    'contract_complete': contract['live_centers'] == 6469 and contract['db_errors'] == 0,
+    'pytest_exit_zero': test['exit_code'] == 0,
+    'head_unchanged': head == '2b1aca18faeef72a5e090dbc3af4ba6151b4ae80',
+    'tracked_and_data_unchanged': not integrity['changed'] and not integrity['added'] and not integrity['tracked_status'].strip(),
+    'no_missing_report_links': not missing,
+    'single_final_verdict': report.rstrip().endswith('VERDICT: APPROVE') and report.count('VERDICT:') == 1,
+    'no_draft_placeholders': not any(x in report for x in ['TODO', 'TBD', 'PLACEHOLDER']),
+}
+ports = [8937] + [int(p) for p in contract['ports_closed']]
+replay_summary = read('replay/execution_summary.json')
+checks['historical_complete'] = replay_summary['leaf_invocations'] == 123 and replay_summary['exit_counts'] == {'0': 123} and not replay_summary['failures'] and not replay_summary['lifecycle_failure_flags']
+for item in replay_summary.get('port_checks', []):
+    ports.append(int(item['port']))
+for key in ['ports_closed', 'ports']:
+    values = replay_summary.get(key, {})
+    if isinstance(values, dict):
+        ports.extend(int(p) for p in values)
+    elif isinstance(values, list):
+        ports.extend(int(p) for p in values if isinstance(p, int) or str(p).isdigit())
+port_results = {}
+for port in sorted(set(ports)):
+    sock = socket.socket()
+    sock.settimeout(.5)
+    port_results[str(port)] = sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1', port)) != 0
+    sock.close()
+checks['checked_ports_closed'] = all(port_results.values())
+result = {'checks': checks, 'missing_links': missing, 'checked_ports_closed': port_results,
+          'report_bytes': (OUT / 'REPORT.md').stat().st_size, 'head': head}
+(OUT / 'report_validation.json').write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=True))
+assert all(checks.values()), result

Each of the seven underscore characters U+005F, U+FE33, U+FE34, U+FE4D, U+FE4E, U+FE4F and U+FF3F inside `(-<character>)-ARTEMISININ` is separately confirmed undrawn over HTTP. |
+| M36-B2 | **FIXED** | `(+/-☢)NICOTINE`, `(±☢)NICOTINE`, `(+/-$)NICOTINE`, `(+/-×)NICOTINE` return `not_one_name`; `(+/☢-)NICOTINE`, `(+/×-)NICOTINE` return `not_found`. All six previously drew CHEMBL440464. Significant symbols remain in the signature. |
+| M36-S1 | **FIXED exact questions** | `What trials evaluate ((+/-))NICOTINE?` and `What does NICOTINE((+/-)) target?`: no linked entity and zero evidence in `/api/ask`, `/api/structured`, and `/api/check`; map `not_found`. Parent reads CHEMBL3 and retrieves two rows. |
+
+Positive controls remain valid: `ARTEMISININ` and `CHEMBL269671` draw CHEMBL269671; `(+/-)NICOTINE`, `( + / - )NICOTINE`, `(+/–)NICOTINE`, `(+/-)nicotine`, fullwidth `（＋/－）NICOTINE`, and accented `(+/-)NÍCOTINE` draw CHEMBL440464. `(±)-nicotine`, `(+-)NICOTINE`, `(-+)-NICOTINE` and `()osimertinib` remain undrawn under the revised policy. [Controls](root_round37_summary.json).
+
+## Independent attack on the two comparison modes
+
+The oracle reconstructs names and candidate IDs from the dictionary and SQL records; it does not use `Engine.names`. Its normalization classifier is a separate character scanner rather than the implementation's `_GROUP` regular expression. [Oracle and source sweep](source/source_audit.py), [composition probes](source/modes.py), [boundary probes](source/mode_boundaries.py).
+
+The critical separation is structural: a strict-mode signature retains at least one opening/initial-quotation and one closing/final-quotation punctuation character. An ordinary-mode signature removes all punctuation. Those signature spaces cannot collide. This argument depends on the promised preprocessing order: trademark removal, compatibility/case folding and mark/invisible removal precede classification; space removal and dash normalization cannot erase those retained delimiters. It supports mode separation, not universal chemical equivalence of ordinary punctuation-normalized names.
+
+- **22,956 source/key inputs × 14 transforms = 321,384 comparisons.** Fourteen literal source spellings use strict mode; 22,942 use ordinary mode. No implementation-versus-independent-oracle mismatch was found. There are 42 mode-dependent punctuation-transform differences, which are expected under the new policy. Two additional generic `swapcase()` invariance probes differ for Turkish dotless `ı`; both agree with the prescribed casefold oracle. Those probe assumptions are not silently counted as passing or as new map defects. [Summary](source/source_mode_summary.json), [differences](source/source_mode_differences.json), [follow-up outcomes](source/mode_followup.json).
+- **11,086 assigned punctuation/mark/symbol/space characters × four contexts = 44,344 cases**, plus all **91 opening × 87 closing delimiters = 7,917 pairs**: zero oracle failures or unsafe plain-ARTEMISININ collisions. [Unicode summary](source/unicode_mode_summary.json).
+- **138,557 assigned Unicode letter/number characters** inserted into a candidate group: zero classifier disagreements. Sixteen lose their letter/number category during the specified compatibility/mark preprocessing and correctly change mode afterwards. [Classification results](source/letter_number_classification.json).
+- Nested and mismatched brackets, Unicode quotations, empty groups, fullwidth forms, signs, unrelated symbols, letters/digits inside a group and preprocessing-created empty groups were exercised. **28 HTTP boundary controls** supplement root's confirmations. `(-ARTEMISININ)` and `(-A)RTEMISININ` have letters inside their only group and therefore use ordinary mode, drawing ARTEMISININ; ASCII apostrophes are category Po, not the designated Pi/Pf quotation delimiters. These results follow the explicit new classifier and inherited ordinary punctuation rule. They are disclosed design boundaries, not evidence that the code interprets arbitrary strings as optical descriptors. [Boundary evidence](source/mode_boundary_http.json).
+- **182 literal refused spellings × 13 variants = 2,366, all refused.** A separate 84 synthetic canonical-only keys × 13 = 1,092 cases yield 1,074 refusals, 12 `alias_only`, six `not_found`, no centers. All **1,066 preferred names × six significant suffixes = 6,396** remain undrawn. **54 private retirement cases / ten assertion groups** pass. [Refusal summary](source/refusal_variants_summary.json), [suffixes](source/significant_suffixes_summary.json), [retirement fixture](source/retired_private_fixture.json).
+
+## New nonblocking findings
+
+### M37-S1 — punctuation or a symbol can end the question descriptor scan before its sign
+
+**Question-linker regression**, `src/query.py:118` and `src/query.py:133–135`. The parent accepted arbitrary nonword characters inside a sign group. The new adjacent run excludes punctuation such as `.` and symbols such as `☢` and `$`. Looking left from NICOTINE, the scan stops on that character before reaching the sign.
+
+| Exact input | Parent question result | HEAD question result | HEAD map |
+|---|---|---|---|
+| `What trials evaluate (+/-.)NICOTINE?` | no entity/evidence | CHEMBL3; two evidence rows | `not_one_name` |
+| `What trials evaluate (+/-☢)NICOTINE?` | no entity/evidence | CHEMBL3; two evidence rows | `not_one_name` |
+| `What trials evaluate (+/-$)NICOTINE?` | no entity/evidence | CHEMBL3; two evidence rows | `not_one_name` |
+| `What trials evaluate NICOTINE(.+/-)?` | no entity/evidence | CHEMBL3; two evidence rows | `not_one_name` |
+
+The same new reading occurs with `×`, `:` and `,`, including mirrored suffix forms. The combined **28-question / 112-HTTP** probe has **12 newly admitted question readings** and no map centers. `/api/ask` and `/api/check` expose the entity; `/api/structured` retrieves the same two evidence rows. The opposite-side punctuation controls remain blocked because the scan meets a sign first. This is a loss of question refusal coverage, not a map admission failure; it is nonblocking under README defect 15 and the explicit review scope. [Prefix/suffix evidence](contract/question_punctuation_regression.json), [mirrored suffix evidence](contract/question_punctuation_mirror.json).
+
+### M37-S2 — ordinary sign-separated lists lose a shared preferred name
+
+**Question recall/design cost**, `src/query.py:133–135`. `What trials evaluate NICOTINE + osimertinib?` previously linked CHEMBL3 and CHEMBL3353410; now only CHEMBL3353410 remains. The selected evidence consequently changes from two NICOTINE rows to 12 osimertinib rows. `What trials evaluate osimertinib + NICOTINE?` and `What trials evaluate NICOTINE - osimertinib?` also drop NICOTINE. The `/` and `and` controls retain both entities.
+
+This follows the requested rule that **any adjacent sign** blocks a shared-key preferred-name reading. It should be understood as a recall tradeoff, rather than claimed to violate that new rule. All such complete questions remain undrawn by the map. The expanded supplement tests **six names × 30 patterns × two wrappers = 360 cases**, with 74 parent-query changes and zero map centers; not all 74 changes are defects. [Supplement and parent comparison](contract/question_supplement.json), [summary](contract/question_supplement_summary.json), [HTTP](contract/question_supplement_http.json).
+
+Previously existing free-text leaks are separate: `What trials evaluate 〔+/-〕NICOTINE?`, `What trials evaluate NICOTINE«+/-»?` and `"What trials evaluate (+/-)\u200bNICOTINE?"` still read CHEMBL3 with two evidence rows, identically to the parent. These remain recorded question-box limitations. Ordinary `(NICOTINE)` recall and the exact M36 nested-descriptor repair pass.
+
+### M37-S3 — page and explore documentation omit the new punctuation exception
+
+**Documentation should-fix**, `web/try.html:54` and `src/app.py:110–115`. Their unqualified claim that punctuation is ignored no longer describes every name. The new `_sig` docstring explains the exception, but the visible page does not. For example, `(+/-)NICOTINE` draws while `(+/-)NICO/TINE`, `(+/-)NICOTINE!`, or bracket changes do not; `()osimertinib` is also undrawn. Explain that a bracketed group without letters/digits switches the whole name to literal-punctuation comparison. These are accepted recall exclusions, so the wording issue does not negate the map safety result.
+

+import importlib.util, json, pathlib, subprocess, sys, urllib.request
+
+ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+OUT = ROOT / 'audit_evidence_claims/m37'
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'src'))
+import app
+
+def save(name, value):
+    (OUT / name).write_text(json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+
+def load_parent(name):
+    dest = OUT / ('parent_' + name + '.py')
+    dest.write_bytes(subprocess.check_output(['git', 'show', 'HEAD^:src/' + name + '.py'], cwd=ROOT))
+    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('m37_parent_' + name, dest)
+    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
+    sys.modules[spec.name] = mod
+    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
+    return mod
+
+parent_query = load_parent('query')
+current_query = sys.modules['query']
+try:
+    sys.modules['query'] = parent_query
+    parent_app = load_parent('app')
+finally:
+    sys.modules['query'] = current_query
+old = parent_app.Engine()
+now = app.Engine()
+
+def compact(body):
+    return {'state': next((k for k in ['center','refused','choose','alias_only','not_one_name','not_found','error'] if k in body), 'question'),
+            'center': body.get('center', {}).get('id'),
+            'choices': [c['id'] for c in body.get('choose', [])],
+            'entities': body.get('entities'), 'evidence_count': len(body.get('evidence', []))}
+
+families = {
+    'M36-B1': ['(-_)-ARTEMISININ', '(_-)-ARTEMISININ', '[-_]-ARTEMISININ',
+               '(-\u200b_\u0301)-ARTEMISININ', '(-\uff3f)-ARTEMISININ', '(-\ufe4d)-ARTEMISININ',
+               '(-())-ARTEMISININ', '\u3014-\u3015-ARTEMISININ', '\u00ab-\u00bb-ARTEMISININ'],

+    'M36-S1': ['What trials evaluate ((+/-))NICOTINE?', 'What does NICOTINE((+/-)) target?'],
+    'M35-B1': ['(-\u200b)-ARTEMISININ', '(-\ufeff)-ARTEMISININ', '(-\u2060)-ARTEMISININ', '(-\u0301)-ARTEMISININ'],
+    'M35-S1': ['What trials evaluate (+/-)(NICOTINE)?', 'What does (+/-)[NICOTINE] target?',
+               'What trials evaluate (NICOTINE)(+/-)?', 'What trials evaluate [NICOTINE](+/-)?'],
+    'descriptor-controls': ['ARTEMISININ', 'CHEMBL269671', '(+/-)NICOTINE', '( + / - )NICOTINE',
+               '(+/\u2013)NICOTINE', '(+/-)nicotine', '(\u00b1)-nicotine', '(+-)NICOTINE',
+               '(-+)-NICOTINE', '(+/-)NICOTINE\u2622', '()osimertinib', '(_-_)_-_P_A_C_L_I_T_A_X_E_L',
+               '(_-_)_-_g_o_s_s_y_p_o_l', '(_-_)_-_s_i_r_p_i_g_l_e_n_a_s_t_a_t'],
+    'mode-boundaries': ['(((-)))ARTEMISININ', '(a(-))ARTEMISININ', '(a-b)ARTEMISININ',
+               '\u2018-\u2019ARTEMISININ', '\u201c-\u201dARTEMISININ',
+               '[)-ARTEMISININ', '(]-ARTEMISININ', '(-\u200b_\u034f)-ARTEMISININ',

+               '(+/-)N\u00cdCOTINE', '(\u200b+/\u0301-)NICOTINE'],
+}
+
+rows = []
+for family, inputs in families.items():
+    for text in inputs:
+        for route in (['/api/explore','/api/ask','/api/structured','/api/check'] if family in ['M36-S1','M35-S1'] else ['/api/explore']):
+            body = {'text': text}
+            if route == '/api/structured': body['answer'] = '{"claims":[]}'
+            if route == '/api/check': body['answer'] = 'NICOTINE is evaluated in trials.'
+            req = urllib.request.Request('http://127.0.0.1:8937' + route, data=json.dumps(body).encode(), headers={'Content-Type':'application/json'})
+            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=180) as r: result = json.load(r); status = r.status
+            rows.append({'family':family, 'input':text, 'escaped':text.encode('unicode_escape').decode(),
+                         'route':route,'request':body,'http_status':status,'response':result,
+                         'sig':app._sig(text),'parent_sig':parent_app._sig(text),

+        save('root_round37_http.json', rows)
+summary = [dict(family=r['family'], input=r['input'], escaped=r['escaped'], route=r['route'],

+                parent_linker=r['parent_linker'], current_linker=r['current_linker']) for r in rows]
+save('root_round37_summary.json', summary)

+import json, pathlib, re, socket, subprocess
+
+ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+OUT = ROOT / 'audit_evidence_claims/m37'
+report = (OUT / 'REPORT.md').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
+missing = []
+link_prose = re.sub(r'```.*?```|`[^`]*`', '', report, flags=re.S)
+for target in re.findall(r'\]\(([^)]+)\)', link_prose):
+    if '://' in target or target.startswith('#'):
+        continue
+    path = target.split('#')[0].strip('<>')
+    if path and not (OUT / path).exists():
+        missing.append(target)
+
+def read(name):
+    return json.loads((OUT / name).read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))
+
+integrity = read('integrity_final.json')
+source = read('source/source_summary.json')
+contract = read('contract/summary.json')
+test = read('pytest_meta.json')
+head = subprocess.check_output(['git', 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], cwd=ROOT).decode().strip()
+checks = {
+    'source_complete': source['source_names'] == 22956 and not source['failures'] and not source['errors'],
+    'contract_complete': contract['live_centers'] == 6469 and contract['db_errors'] == 0,
+    'pytest_exit_zero': test['exit_code'] == 0,
+    'head_unchanged': head == '2b1aca18faeef72a5e090dbc3af4ba6151b4ae80',
+    'tracked_and_data_unchanged': not integrity['changed'] and not integrity['added'] and not integrity['tracked_status'].strip(),
+    'no_missing_report_links': not missing,
+    'single_final_verdict': report.rstrip().endswith('VERDICT: APPROVE') and report.count('VERDICT:') == 1,
+    'no_draft_placeholders': not any(x in report for x in ['TODO', 'TBD', 'PLACEHOLDER']),
+}
+ports = [8937] + [int(p) for p in contract['ports_closed']]
+replay_summary = read('replay/execution_summary.json')
+checks['historical_complete'] = replay_summary['leaf_invocations'] == 123 and replay_summary['exit_counts'] == {'0': 123} and not replay_summary['failures'] and not replay_summary['lifecycle_failure_flags']
+for item in replay_summary.get('port_checks', []):
+    ports.append(int(item['port']))
+for key in ['ports_closed', 'ports']:
+    values = replay_summary.get(key, {})
+    if isinstance(values, dict):
+        ports.extend(int(p) for p in values)
+    elif isinstance(values, list):
+        ports.extend(int(p) for p in values if isinstance(p, int) or str(p).isdigit())
+port_results = {}
+for port in sorted(set(ports)):
+    sock = socket.socket()
+    sock.settimeout(.5)
+    port_results[str(port)] = sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1', port)) != 0
+    sock.close()
+checks['checked_ports_closed'] = all(port_results.values())
+result = {'checks': checks, 'missing_links': missing, 'checked_ports_closed': port_results,
+          'report_bytes': (OUT / 'REPORT.md').stat().st_size, 'head': head}
+(OUT / 'report_validation.json').write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=True))
+assert all(checks.values()), result
